	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ind value = 16 } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:gsm_t_site = { is_variable_set = gsm_sd_security } } set_variable = { which = gsm_t_rsx value = event_target:gsm_exchange.gsm_month } subtract_variable = { which = gsm_t_rsx value = event_target:gsm_t_site.gsm_sd_security } if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_rsx value < @gsm_resale_months } } set_variable = { which = gsm_t_rsp value = @gsm_resale_premium } } }
	}
}
gsm_gen_site_jf = {
	set_variable = { which = gsm_t_sjf value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ind value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:gsm_t_site = { is_variable_set = gsm_jf_energy } } set_variable = { which = gsm_t_sjf value = event_target:gsm_t_site.gsm_jf_energy } }
